- **ci**: How the [Vaked agent fleet](../../VAKED_AGENTS.md) is wired into GitHub Actions: triggers, the shared `ci` environment, secrets, the build pipeline, and the conventions every agent workflow follows. All workflows live in [`.github/workflows/`](../../.github/workflows). ([peterlodri-sec/vaked-base](https://github.com/peterlodri-sec/vaked-base/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ci.md) · ★3)
- **ci**: The automatic quality workflows use read-only tokens, job timeouts and per-workflow/ref concurrency with `cancel-in-progress: true`. Push checks run only on `main`; feature branches are checked through their PR, avoiding duplicate feature push and PR runs. A post-merge `main` run verifies the integrated commit and is separate acceptance evidence. ([automatedigital/spark](https://github.com/automatedigital/spark/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ci.md) · ★2)
- **ci**: - **`pnpm verify:full` is the local gate, and its last line is the answer.** It runs `verify` then Chromium e2e. CI is the proof: a ready pull request runs `verify` and Playwright on Chromium, Firefox, and WebKit in parallel. There is no pre-push hook. `pnpm verify` is the browser-free chain inside the local command, so `e2e/**` is invisible to `verify` alone. `verify:full` prints one verdict line ([freegantt/freegantt](https://github.com/freegantt/freegantt/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ci.md) · ★2)
- **ci**: Generate and improve CI/CD workflows. ([himanshu231204/agent-skills](https://github.com/himanshu231204/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/skills/contributor-ready/agents/ci.md) · ★2)
- **ci**: Change CI, a GitHub workflow, the deploy, a git hook, a shell script, or the repo's own gates and tooling under `scripts/` and `build/` — the pipeline and its settings (gate ordering, deploy conditions, retry counts), not the specs it runs, which the `e2e` agent owns, nor the database and in-server harnesses (`test-endpoints`, `test-schema`, `test-migrations` and their `scripts/lib/` stand-ins), which the `integration` agent owns. Named for the Four Sages of Dwartii, ancient lawgivers whose statues stand in the office of a man who ignored them: a gate that judges nothing is worse than no gate… ([quissicutdeus/mica](https://github.com/quissicutdeus/mica/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ci.md) · ★2)
- **ci**: How CI is shaped, as opposed to what it checks — the gate's tools are in [`lint-gate.md`](lint-gate.md) and the suite is in [`testing.md`](testing.md).
